# Graph Report - Reasoner  (2026-06-12)

## Corpus Check
- 2762 files · ~2,699,115 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 32159 nodes · 39544 edges · 2501 communities (2415 shown, 86 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4822 edges (avg confidence: 0.55)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a027bee5`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
